2.1 · deliver：参数与依据
{}

execution_model / EM10

Deterministic processing compilation: model mode declares model processing and requires llm among participating operators. Before model processing, observe its actually referenced external input versions; intermediate values computed within that same segment are not fresh external inputs; acquired read/receive outputs become observable after acquisition. Default mode retains possible observation of read/receive outputs and non-isolated transformation inputs; it does not observe receive request parameters merely because a response is acquired. Local mode requires explicit source or code/interface evidence and an explicit returns list; internal reads are not model observations, while declared returned values cross to the model after the local segment. Empty local returns means no content returned to the model by that segment. Deduplicate unchanged external symbolic references within one processing segment by value reference and its consumption condition. A conditional observation never subsumes a later unconditional consumption. Conditions needed for model processing are themselves observed when applicable; a definitely omitted build member consumes no member value. Preserve earlier whole-value observation even when a later request member is omitted. Preserve different segments and returned/acquired versions, without treating every intermediate computation as another model call. Generated model-observation effects do not automatically add llm to operator or sink to roles; retain the independently evidenced action labels. The compiler creates these observations from typed modes and references, not opcode or evidence keywords. A model may still misclassify a mode; compilation proves structural implications of the supplied mode, not its truth. IR order is fixed or partial. For partial order, preserve required definition/use and declared within-segment ordering; the compiler records observation-before-model-processing, acquisition-before-observation, and local-processing-before-return-observation constraints. Independent segments may interleave between primitive operations. Partial order is a computable may relation, not a semantic failure.

程序按已声明处理模式及实际符号引用编译观察；不证明模式判断正确或真实执行已发生。

2.1 · deliver：参数与依据
{}

execution_model / EM10

Deterministic processing compilation: model mode declares model processing and requires llm among participating operators. Before model processing, observe its actually referenced external input versions; intermediate values computed within that same segment are not fresh external inputs; acquired read/receive outputs become observable after acquisition. Default mode retains possible observation of read/receive outputs and non-isolated transformation inputs; it does not observe receive request parameters merely because a response is acquired. Local mode requires explicit source or code/interface evidence and an explicit returns list; internal reads are not model observations, while declared returned values cross to the model after the local segment. Empty local returns means no content returned to the model by that segment. Deduplicate unchanged external symbolic references within one processing segment by value reference and its consumption condition. A conditional observation never subsumes a later unconditional consumption. Conditions needed for model processing are themselves observed when applicable; a definitely omitted build member consumes no member value. Preserve earlier whole-value observation even when a later request member is omitted. Preserve different segments and returned/acquired versions, without treating every intermediate computation as another model call. Generated model-observation effects do not automatically add llm to operator or sink to roles; retain the independently evidenced action labels. The compiler creates these observations from typed modes and references, not opcode or evidence keywords. A model may still misclassify a mode; compilation proves structural implications of the supplied mode, not its truth. IR order is fixed or partial. For partial order, preserve required definition/use and declared within-segment ordering; the compiler records observation-before-model-processing, acquisition-before-observation, and local-processing-before-return-observation constraints. Independent segments may interleave between primitive operations. Partial order is a computable may relation, not a semantic failure.

程序按已声明处理模式及实际符号引用编译观察；不证明模式判断正确或真实执行已发生。

1.1 · deliver：参数与依据
{}

execution_model / EM10

Deterministic processing compilation: model mode declares model processing and requires llm among participating operators. Before model processing, observe its actually referenced external input versions; intermediate values computed within that same segment are not fresh external inputs; acquired read/receive outputs become observable after acquisition. Default mode retains possible observation of read/receive outputs and non-isolated transformation inputs; it does not observe receive request parameters merely because a response is acquired. Local mode requires explicit source or code/interface evidence and an explicit returns list; internal reads are not model observations, while declared returned values cross to the model after the local segment. Empty local returns means no content returned to the model by that segment. Deduplicate unchanged external symbolic references within one processing segment by value reference and its consumption condition. A conditional observation never subsumes a later unconditional consumption. Conditions needed for model processing are themselves observed when applicable; a definitely omitted build member consumes no member value. Preserve earlier whole-value observation even when a later request member is omitted. Preserve different segments and returned/acquired versions, without treating every intermediate computation as another model call. Generated model-observation effects do not automatically add llm to operator or sink to roles; retain the independently evidenced action labels. The compiler creates these observations from typed modes and references, not opcode or evidence keywords. A model may still misclassify a mode; compilation proves structural implications of the supplied mode, not its truth. IR order is fixed or partial. For partial order, preserve required definition/use and declared within-segment ordering; the compiler records observation-before-model-processing, acquisition-before-observation, and local-processing-before-return-observation constraints. Independent segments may interleave between primitive operations. Partial order is a computable may relation, not a semantic failure.

程序按已声明处理模式及实际符号引用编译观察；不证明模式判断正确或真实执行已发生。

execution_model / EM10

Deterministic processing compilation: model mode declares model processing and requires llm among participating operators. Before model processing, observe its actually referenced external input versions; intermediate values computed within that same segment are not fresh external inputs; acquired read/receive outputs become observable after acquisition. Default mode retains possible observation of read/receive outputs and non-isolated transformation inputs; it does not observe receive request parameters merely because a response is acquired. Local mode requires explicit source or code/interface evidence and an explicit returns list; internal reads are not model observations, while declared returned values cross to the model after the local segment. Empty local returns means no content returned to the model by that segment. Deduplicate unchanged external symbolic references within one processing segment by value reference and its consumption condition. A conditional observation never subsumes a later unconditional consumption. Conditions needed for model processing are themselves observed when applicable; a definitely omitted build member consumes no member value. Preserve earlier whole-value observation even when a later request member is omitted. Preserve different segments and returned/acquired versions, without treating every intermediate computation as another model call. Generated model-observation effects do not automatically add llm to operator or sink to roles; retain the independently evidenced action labels. The compiler creates these observations from typed modes and references, not opcode or evidence keywords. A model may still misclassify a mode; compilation proves structural implications of the supplied mode, not its truth. IR order is fixed or partial. For partial order, preserve required definition/use and declared within-segment ordering; the compiler records observation-before-model-processing, acquisition-before-observation, and local-processing-before-return-observation constraints. Independent segments may interleave between primitive operations. Partial order is a computable may relation, not a semantic failure.

程序按已声明处理模式及实际符号引用编译观察；不证明模式判断正确或真实执行已发生。

execution_model / EM02

content returned by an agent tool is retained as possibly entering the next model request unless an explicit content-routing or isolation mechanism restricts it

工具返回内容默认可能进入后续模型请求；未见隔离或限定路由机制，按默认模式标注。

execution_model / EM09

known tool acquisition without established networking uses receive at a tool location in a null-effect event

fast.fetch 是已知工具但无网络传输机制证据，响应按工具边界获取。

  "evidences": [
    {
      "basis": "execution_model",

effects · 效果索引 0 · model_observe

execution_model / EM10

Deterministic processing compilation: model mode declares model processing and requires llm among participating operators. Before model processing, observe its actually referenced external input versions; intermediate values computed within that same segment are not fresh external inputs; acquired read/receive outputs become observable after acquisition. Default mode retains possible observation of read/receive outputs and non-isolated transformation inputs; it does not observe receive request parameters merely because a response is acquired. Local mode requires explicit source or code/interface evidence and an explicit returns list; internal reads are not model observations, while declared returned values cross to the model after the local segment. Empty local returns means no content returned to the model by that segment. Deduplicate unchanged external symbolic references within one processing segment by value reference and its consumption condition. A conditional observation never subsumes a later unconditional consumption. Conditions needed for model processing are themselves observed when applicable; a definitely omitted build member consumes no member value. Preserve earlier whole-value observation even when a later request member is omitted. Preserve different segments and returned/acquired versions, without treating every intermediate computation as another model call. Generated model-observation effects do not automatically add llm to operator or sink to roles; retain the independently evidenced action labels. The compiler creates these observations from typed modes and references, not opcode or evidence keywords. A model may still misclassify a mode; compilation proves structural implications of the supplied mode, not its truth. IR order is fixed or partial. For partial order, preserve required definition/use and declared within-segment ordering; the compiler records observation-before-model-processing, acquisition-before-observation, and local-processing-before-return-observation constraints. Independent segments may interleave between primitive operations. Partial order is a computable may relation, not a semantic failure.

程序按已声明处理模式及实际符号引用编译观察；不证明模式判断正确或真实执行已发生。

原标注审计：规格、输出绑定及 Security Profile 依据
1.1 · deliver：参数与依据
{}

execution_model / EM10

Deterministic processing compilation: model mode declares model processing and requires llm among participating operators. Before model processing, observe its actually referenced external input versions; intermediate values computed within that same segment are not fresh external inputs; acquired read/receive outputs become observable after acquisition. Default mode retains possible observation of read/receive outputs and non-isolated transformation inputs; it does not observe receive request parameters merely because a response is acquired. Local mode requires explicit source or code/interface evidence and an explicit returns list; internal reads are not model observations, while declared returned values cross to the model after the local segment. Empty local returns means no content returned to the model by that segment. Deduplicate unchanged external symbolic references within one processing segment by value reference and its consumption condition. A conditional observation never subsumes a later unconditional consumption. Conditions needed for model processing are themselves observed when applicable; a definitely omitted build member consumes no member value. Preserve earlier whole-value observation even when a later request member is omitted. Preserve different segments and returned/acquired versions, without treating every intermediate computation as another model call. Generated model-observation effects do not automatically add llm to operator or sink to roles; retain the independently evidenced action labels. The compiler creates these observations from typed modes and references, not opcode or evidence keywords. A model may still misclassify a mode; compilation proves structural implications of the supplied mode, not its truth. IR order is fixed or partial. For partial order, preserve required definition/use and declared within-segment ordering; the compiler records observation-before-model-processing, acquisition-before-observation, and local-processing-before-return-observation constraints. Independent segments may interleave between primitive operations. Partial order is a computable may relation, not a semantic failure.

程序按已声明处理模式及实际符号引用编译观察；不证明模式判断正确或真实执行已发生。

source / src_003

Retry fast.fetch exactly once only when its first attempt fails with a transient error

对首次响应分类的处理没有模型或本地隔离证据，按默认模式标注。

execution_model / EM10

Local mode requires explicit source or code/interface evidence and an explicit returns list

缺少本地隔离机制证据，不能声明 local 模式；默认模式保留响应版本的可能观察。

对首次响应做结果分类由运行时执行，用于区分成功、瞬时失败与非瞬时失败。

roles · transformer

source / src_003

return that successful response's body value unchanged

对响应内容分类并选出 body 字段，属于对内容的处理。

effects · 效果索引 0 · model_observe

execution_model / EM10

Deterministic processing compilation: model mode declares model processing and requires llm among participating operators. Before model processing, observe its actually referenced external input versions; intermediate values computed within that same segment are not fresh external inputs; acquired read/receive outputs become observable after acquisition. Default mode retains possible observation of read/receive outputs and non-isolated transformation inputs; it does not observe receive request parameters merely because a response is acquired. Local mode requires explicit source or code/interface evidence and an explicit returns list; internal reads are not model observations, while declared returned values cross to the model after the local segment. Empty local returns means no content returned to the model by that segment. Deduplicate unchanged external symbolic references within one processing segment by value reference and its consumption condition. A conditional observation never subsumes a later unconditional consumption. Conditions needed for model processing are themselves observed when applicable; a definitely omitted build member consumes no member value. Preserve earlier whole-value observation even when a later request member is omitted. Preserve different segments and returned/acquired versions, without treating every intermediate computation as another model call. Generated model-observation effects do not automatically add llm to operator or sink to roles; retain the independently evidenced action labels. The compiler creates these observations from typed modes and references, not opcode or evidence keywords. A model may still misclassify a mode; compilation proves structural implications of the supplied mode, not its truth. IR order is fixed or partial. For partial order, preserve required definition/use and declared within-segment ordering; the compiler records observation-before-model-processing, acquisition-before-observation, and local-processing-before-return-observation constraints. Independent segments may interleave between primitive operations. Partial order is a computable may relation, not a semantic failure.

程序按已声明处理模式及实际符号引用编译观察；不证明模式判断正确或真实执行已发生。

effects · 效果索引 0 · model_observe

source / src_003

对首次响应分类的处理没有模型或本地隔离证据，按默认模式标注。

effects · 效果索引 0 · model_observe

缺少本地隔离机制证据，不能声明 local 模式；默认模式保留响应版本的可能观察。

execution_model / EM10

Deterministic processing compilation: model mode declares model processing and requires llm among participating operators. Before model processing, observe its actually referenced external input versions; intermediate values computed within that same segment are not fresh external inputs; acquired read/receive outputs become observable after acquisition. Default mode retains possible observation of read/receive outputs and non-isolated transformation inputs; it does not observe receive request parameters merely because a response is acquired. Local mode requires explicit source or code/interface evidence and an explicit returns list; internal reads are not model observations, while declared returned values cross to the model after the local segment. Empty local returns means no content returned to the model by that segment. Deduplicate unchanged external symbolic references within one processing segment by value reference and its consumption condition. A conditional observation never subsumes a later unconditional consumption. Conditions needed for model processing are themselves observed when applicable; a definitely omitted build member consumes no member value. Preserve earlier whole-value observation even when a later request member is omitted. Preserve different segments and returned/acquired versions, without treating every intermediate computation as another model call. Generated model-observation effects do not automatically add llm to operator or sink to roles; retain the independently evidenced action labels. The compiler creates these observations from typed modes and references, not opcode or evidence keywords. A model may still misclassify a mode; compilation proves structural implications of the supplied mode, not its truth. IR order is fixed or partial. For partial order, preserve required definition/use and declared within-segment ordering; the compiler records observation-before-model-processing, acquisition-before-observation, and local-processing-before-return-observation constraints. Independent segments may interleave between primitive operations. Partial order is a computable may relation, not a semantic failure.

程序按已声明处理模式及实际符号引用编译观察；不证明模式判断正确或真实执行已发生。

effects · 效果索引 0 · model_observe

execution_model / EM02

content returned by an agent tool is retained as possibly entering the next model request unless an explicit content-routing or isolation mechanism restricts it

重试返回内容默认可能进入后续模型请求，未见隔离或限定路由机制。

fast.fetch 为已知工具但无网络传输机制证据，重试响应按工具边界获取。

effects · 效果索引 0 · model_observe

execution_model / EM10

Deterministic processing compilation: model mode declares model processing and requires llm among participating operators. Before model processing, observe its actually referenced external input versions; intermediate values computed within that same segment are not fresh external inputs; acquired read/receive outputs become observable after acquisition. Default mode retains possible observation of read/receive outputs and non-isolated transformation inputs; it does not observe receive request parameters merely because a response is acquired. Local mode requires explicit source or code/interface evidence and an explicit returns list; internal reads are not model observations, while declared returned values cross to the model after the local segment. Empty local returns means no content returned to the model by that segment. Deduplicate unchanged external symbolic references within one processing segment by value reference and its consumption condition. A conditional observation never subsumes a later unconditional consumption. Conditions needed for model processing are themselves observed when applicable; a definitely omitted build member consumes no member value. Preserve earlier whole-value observation even when a later request member is omitted. Preserve different segments and returned/acquired versions, without treating every intermediate computation as another model call. Generated model-observation effects do not automatically add llm to operator or sink to roles; retain the independently evidenced action labels. The compiler creates these observations from typed modes and references, not opcode or evidence keywords. A model may still misclassify a mode; compilation proves structural implications of the supplied mode, not its truth. IR order is fixed or partial. For partial order, preserve required definition/use and declared within-segment ordering; the compiler records observation-before-model-processing, acquisition-before-observation, and local-processing-before-return-observation constraints. Independent segments may interleave between primitive operations. Partial order is a computable may relation, not a semantic failure.

程序按已声明处理模式及实际符号引用编译观察；不证明模式判断正确或真实执行已发生。

effects · 效果索引 0 · model_observe

对重试响应分类的处理无模型或本地隔离证据，按默认模式标注。

缺少本地隔离机制证据，不能声明 local 模式。

2.1 · deliver：参数与依据
{}

execution_model / EM10

Deterministic processing compilation: model mode declares model processing and requires llm among participating operators. Before model processing, observe its actually referenced external input versions; intermediate values computed within that same segment are not fresh external inputs; acquired read/receive outputs become observable after acquisition. Default mode retains possible observation of read/receive outputs and non-isolated transformation inputs; it does not observe receive request parameters merely because a response is acquired. Local mode requires explicit source or code/interface evidence and an explicit returns list; internal reads are not model observations, while declared returned values cross to the model after the local segment. Empty local returns means no content returned to the model by that segment. Deduplicate unchanged external symbolic references within one processing segment by value reference and its consumption condition. A conditional observation never subsumes a later unconditional consumption. Conditions needed for model processing are themselves observed when applicable; a definitely omitted build member consumes no member value. Preserve earlier whole-value observation even when a later request member is omitted. Preserve different segments and returned/acquired versions, without treating every intermediate computation as another model call. Generated model-observation effects do not automatically add llm to operator or sink to roles; retain the independently evidenced action labels. The compiler creates these observations from typed modes and references, not opcode or evidence keywords. A model may still misclassify a mode; compilation proves structural implications of the supplied mode, not its truth. IR order is fixed or partial. For partial order, preserve required definition/use and declared within-segment ordering; the compiler records observation-before-model-processing, acquisition-before-observation, and local-processing-before-return-observation constraints. Independent segments may interleave between primitive operations. Partial order is a computable may relation, not a semantic failure.

程序按已声明处理模式及实际符号引用编译观察；不证明模式判断正确或真实执行已发生。

execution_model / EM02

content returned by an agent tool is retained as possibly entering the next model request unless an explicit content-routing or isolation mechanism restricts it

工具返回内容默认可能进入后续模型请求，未见隔离或限定路由机制。

execution_model / EM09

known tool acquisition without established networking uses receive at a tool location in a null-effect event

archive.fetch 部署方式未说明且无网络传输证据，响应按工具边界获取。

{
  "evidences": [
    {
      "basis": "execution_model",
      "quote": "Deterministic processing compilation: model mode declares model processing and requires llm among participating operators. Before model processing, observe its actually referenced external input versions; intermediate values computed within that same segment are not fresh external inputs; acquired read/receive outputs become observable after acquisition. Default mode retains possible observation of read/receive outputs and non-isolated transformation inputs; it does not observe receive request parameters merely because a response is acquired. Local mode requires explicit source or code/interface evidence and an explicit returns list; internal reads are not model observations, while declared returned values cross to the model after the local segment. Empty local returns means no content returned to the model by that segment. Deduplicate unchanged external symbolic references within one processing segment by value reference and its consumption condition. A conditional observation never subsumes a later unconditional consumption. Conditions needed for model processing are themselves observed when applicable; a definitely omitted build member consumes no member value. Preserve earlier whole-value observation even when a later request member is omitted. Preserve different segments and returned/acquired versions, without treating every intermediate computation as another model call. Generated model-observation effects do not automatically add llm to operator or sink to roles; retain the independently evidenced action labels. The compiler creates these observations from typed modes and references, not opcode or evidence keywords. A model may still misclassify a mode; compilation proves structural implications of the supplied mode, not its truth. IR order is fixed or partial. For partial order, preserve required definition/use and declared within-segment ordering; the compiler records observation-before-model-processing, acquisition-before-observation, and local-processing-before-return-observation constraints. Independent segments may interleave between primitive operations. Partial order is a computable may relation, not a semantic failure.",

effects · 效果索引 0 · model_observe

execution_model / EM10

Deterministic processing compilation: model mode declares model processing and requires llm among participating operators. Before model processing, observe its actually referenced external input versions; intermediate values computed within that same segment are not fresh external inputs; acquired read/receive outputs become observable after acquisition. Default mode retains possible observation of read/receive outputs and non-isolated transformation inputs; it does not observe receive request parameters merely because a response is acquired. Local mode requires explicit source or code/interface evidence and an explicit returns list; internal reads are not model observations, while declared returned values cross to the model after the local segment. Empty local returns means no content returned to the model by that segment. Deduplicate unchanged external symbolic references within one processing segment by value reference and its consumption condition. A conditional observation never subsumes a later unconditional consumption. Conditions needed for model processing are themselves observed when applicable; a definitely omitted build member consumes no member value. Preserve earlier whole-value observation even when a later request member is omitted. Preserve different segments and returned/acquired versions, without treating every intermediate computation as another model call. Generated model-observation effects do not automatically add llm to operator or sink to roles; retain the independently evidenced action labels. The compiler creates these observations from typed modes and references, not opcode or evidence keywords. A model may still misclassify a mode; compilation proves structural implications of the supplied mode, not its truth. IR order is fixed or partial. For partial order, preserve required definition/use and declared within-segment ordering; the compiler records observation-before-model-processing, acquisition-before-observation, and local-processing-before-return-observation constraints. Independent segments may interleave between primitive operations. Partial order is a computable may relation, not a semantic failure.

程序按已声明处理模式及实际符号引用编译观察；不证明模式判断正确或真实执行已发生。

effects · 效果索引 0 · model_observe

execution_model / EM02

content returned by an agent tool is retained as possibly entering the next model request unless an explicit content-routing or isolation mechanism restricts it

工具返回内容默认可能进入后续模型请求，未见隔离或限定路由机制。

effects · 效果索引 0 · model_observe

execution_model / EM09

known tool acquisition without established networking uses receive at a tool location in a null-effect event

archive.fetch 部署方式未说明且无网络传输证据，响应按工具边界获取。

原标注审计：规格、输出绑定及 Security Profile 依据
1.1 · deliver：参数与依据
{}

execution_model / EM10

Deterministic processing compilation: model mode declares model processing and requires llm among participating operators. Before model processing, observe its actually referenced external input versions; intermediate values computed within that same segment are not fresh external inputs; acquired read/receive outputs become observable after acquisition. Default mode retains possible observation of read/receive outputs and non-isolated transformation inputs; it does not observe receive request parameters merely because a response is acquired. Local mode requires explicit source or code/interface evidence and an explicit returns list; internal reads are not model observations, while declared returned values cross to the model after the local segment. Empty local returns means no content returned to the model by that segment. Deduplicate unchanged external symbolic references within one processing segment by value reference and its consumption condition. A conditional observation never subsumes a later unconditional consumption. Conditions needed for model processing are themselves observed when applicable; a definitely omitted build member consumes no member value. Preserve earlier whole-value observation even when a later request member is omitted. Preserve different segments and returned/acquired versions, without treating every intermediate computation as another model call. Generated model-observation effects do not automatically add llm to operator or sink to roles; retain the independently evidenced action labels. The compiler creates these observations from typed modes and references, not opcode or evidence keywords. A model may still misclassify a mode; compilation proves structural implications of the supplied mode, not its truth. IR order is fixed or partial. For partial order, preserve required definition/use and declared within-segment ordering; the compiler records observation-before-model-processing, acquisition-before-observation, and local-processing-before-return-observation constraints. Independent segments may interleave between primitive operations. Partial order is a computable may relation, not a semantic failure.

程序按已声明处理模式及实际符号引用编译观察；不证明模式判断正确或真实执行已发生。

对响应分类与提取的处理没有模型或本地隔离证据，按默认模式标注。

__compiled_model_context__ · model_context: 当前模型处理上下文

[]

execution_model / EM10

Deterministic processing compilation: model mode declares model processing and requires llm among participating operators. Before model processing, observe its actually referenced external input versions; intermediate values computed within that same segment are not fresh external inputs; acquired read/receive outputs become observable after acquisition. Default mode retains possible observation of read/receive outputs and non-isolated transformation inputs; it does not observe receive request parameters merely because a response is acquired. Local mode requires explicit source or code/interface evidence and an explicit returns list; internal reads are not model observations, while declared returned values cross to the model after the local segment. Empty local returns means no content returned to the model by that segment. Deduplicate unchanged external symbolic references within one processing segment by value reference and its consumption condition. A conditional observation never subsumes a later unconditional consumption. Conditions needed for model processing are themselves observed when applicable; a definitely omitted build member consumes no member value. Preserve earlier whole-value observation even when a later request member is omitted. Preserve different segments and returned/acquired versions, without treating every intermediate computation as another model call. Generated model-observation effects do not automatically add llm to operator or sink to roles; retain the independently evidenced action labels. The compiler creates these observations from typed modes and references, not opcode or evidence keywords. A model may still misclassify a mode; compilation proves structural implications of the supplied mode, not its truth. IR order is fixed or partial. For partial order, preserve required definition/use and declared within-segment ordering; the compiler records observation-before-model-processing, acquisition-before-observation, and local-processing-before-return-observation constraints. Independent segments may interleave between primitive operations. Partial order is a computable may relation, not a semantic failure.

程序按已声明处理模式及实际符号引用编译观察；不证明模式判断正确或真实执行已发生。

execution_model / EM12

Boundary properties and defaults: access_scope is task, recipient, shared, or public; retention is task, persistent, or null when a receiving boundary's retention is not modeled. Storage and runtime-context locations require non-null retention. A task-internal tool requires supported local execution and task-internal receiving scope; explicitly nonlocal tools use recipient. Unspecified tool deployment conservatively retains an external recipient possibility, without inventing net_send or asserting measured remote execution. Runtime context defaults to task access and task retention; explicit cross-task memory or cross-subject sharing changes the respective property. Files default to task access and persistent retention; task-limited retention requires an explicit temporary-use and cleanup mechanism, not a temp-sounding name. Model, remote, and user boundaries default to recipient access, with null retention unless explicit retention is supported. A locally hosted model remains a content-processing recipient. Public access and sharing require source, code, interface, or supported contract evidence. Null retention does not establish no saving, logging, or future reuse; do not invent unmentioned backend persistence. These properties characterize static boundaries, not sensitivity, necessity, risk, or execution success.

程序生成的模型接收边界使用 recipient；未建模保存期限保持 null，不声明服务端不保存。
